# Independent repository reviews

This ledger covers 525 catalog-owned repository reviews. Evidence levels apply to each classification. These entries have not been compiled or run for this catalog; imported playground verification remains separate.

Generated by `python xreviews.py` from `data/local.json`.

## 2DECOMP Fourier fork

**J — documentation-reviewed**

The pinned README describes inherited 2DECOMP parallel FFT functionality. The older discovery-list wording about further transform extensions is not used to assert unreviewed algorithms; this snapshot is distinguished from the separately maintained upstream library.

Reviewed 2026-11-04 at `a7655268d78937f7b65043871af3d23736c55845`.

[README](https://github.com/BenMql/2decomp_emptyFourierFourier/blob/a7655268d78937f7b65043871af3d23736c55845/README)
SHA-256 (normalized UTF-8/LF): `35fd10bb370405a121c0bc2b8c7a5e63b0192233960c24290e3d9f9e6fcfa194`.

Not built or run for this catalog. Broad GAMS divisions are assigned first; granular classes and procedure mappings require a later individual review. Build systems are recorded only where reviewed documentation identifies them; an empty list means not yet reviewed.

[Pinned discovery list](https://github.com/Beliavsky/Fortran-code-on-GitHub/blob/f5ab25294a12ed004d5a8934200ca8e26ac2e825/README.md).
